# Journey durations

Band ratio: 1.265340. Inner ceiling: 5000 ms.

Only passed assertions contribute durations. A failed run remains in the header. Repeated --run paths count once. Inputs are caller-selected; this instrument does not verify tree identity or lock ownership.

R3 = ceil(max × max(band, ratio) / 100) × 100 ms for settle readings only. R4 = ceil(max × max(band, ratio) / 1000) × 1000 + ceiling ms. Figures need at least two distinct eligible runs and a positive minimum. Slack = supplied timeout minus the title maximum across variants. Out-of-band runs and omitted run/title pairs contribute no readings. Command readings carry no load and take no R5 test.

R5 test: flag when the title ratio exceeds the band and there exists an eligible pair with slow/fast > band but outside(slow) <= outside(fast). The pair is printed as the witness. This conservative inversion test is diagnostic, not proof of a cause; an unmarked title is not cleared of timing defects. R5 figures are provisional and await diagnosis.

Unspecified means no variant key was present. Unattributed means the title repeats without a usable variant key; its pooled range is descriptive and cannot size an individual variant. Timing entries identify unique titles; for repeated all-passed titles their own milliseconds appear in separate variant rows. Report array order is never a variant key.

The slowest `the header buttons finish their color transitions` reading is 2.5 ms in task75-u7b-header-2 (seconds=151.503, no load reading). Its family-specific R3 figure is 100 ms and its enclosing test has 10396.7 ms slack.

U8's budgeted waitForCondition must cover the header color-transition predicate (tests/app/browser/Showcase.test.ts:416), resized-header scroll offset (:707), settled contents heading (:718), and removed-offset control (:753). Their observed maxima are 2.5, 0.3, 712.1, and 698.7 ms. The body animation wait reached through applyFace/applyTheme (tests/setupBrowser.ts:1594) and the banner animation waits in readShowcaseChrome (:1949 and :1971) need the animation budget as ruled. The open/closed dialog status waits (Showcase.test.ts:851 and :856) are also recorded, but are not header waits.

## Slack findings and instrument limitations

The generated section has slack values for every journey title and every supplied Showcase title. No journey title has slack under 10000 ms. The minimum journey slack is 10140.4 ms for `showcase journeys J1 arrives on the showcase and reads its header, state, and contents`, with a maximum of 4859.6 ms in task75-u5-journey-1.

The Showcase titles with slack under 10000 ms are:

| Title | Slack ms |
| --- | ---: |
| Showcase holds each z-index panel at half its stage under every face | 9460.0 |
| Showcase paints and clears the mapped focus halo through Tab under every face and theme | 8550.3 |
| Showcase changes alignment at each mapped sm boundary under every face | 4934.5 |
| Showcase moves between every pair of faces through the Stylesheets buttons | 7132.6 |

The unmodified duration instrument emits 814 statechart settle rows, no `R3 — not below slack` marks, and 569 unavailable R3 figures. The figure requires a positive minimum and two eligible runs. Its largest available statechart R3 figure is 82100 ms for offcanvas motion=false / Open the start panel completes 0:{Enter}, against 202713.6 ms slack. This is not a proposed shared COMPONENT_WAIT budget.

durations.ts:81 constructs a statechart title for every family. durations.ts:401 then requires that constructed title to match a passed assertion. Consequently it omits the actual Showcase header tests and U7a's other non-statechart tests from its settle section, although it prints their test slacks. The following explicitly labelled supplement resolves each short family against its unique full assertion title, reads stdout.log once, and applies the same R3 formula, positive-minimum rule, two-run rule, supplied timeouts, and band. It changes neither the instrument nor its generated rows. The full supplemental population follows. Its marks are:

| Enclosing test | Description | R3 ms | Slack ms | Result |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| showcase journeys paints progress bars at their announced fraction under every face | animations body. | 752100 | 109087.8 | R3 — not below slack |
| showcase journeys J8 drives the engine through the component sections and opens nothing on arrival | animations body. | 778200 | 99449.0 | R3 — not below slack |
| showcase journeys J8 drives the engine through the component sections and opens nothing on arrival | animations body.modal-open | 310500 | 99449.0 | R3 — not below slack |
| showcase matrix compares paired open engine states under bootstrap, unexcluded, and tailwindcss, reads the documented collapse departure, and switches a shown popover | Customs status description is hidden | 50900 | 27945.3 | R3 — not below slack |
| Showcase keeps the compact sticky header neutral under every face at narrow and wide viewports | animations header.position-sticky top-0 z-3 bg-body border-bottom | 151100 | 28495.7 | R3 — not below slack |

These marks prevent claiming that every derived wait figure fits its enclosing slack. One unverified hypothesis: a shared description pools no-animation or already-settled calls with waits that actually span a transition, making max/min a poor measure of between-run variation. This report applies the specified formula without changing the margin or selecting a COMPONENT_WAIT budget.

## Timeout provenance

All source citations name the restored 6a976a0 probe checkout. The journey wrapper has no testTimeout (configs/app/vite.journey.config.ts:18); appJourney inherits appBrowser (vite.config.ts:398), which also sets none (vite.config.ts:298). Titles without a third argument take 15000 ms from [installed Vitest browser default](/home/user/.wave/veneer-u7b/node_modules/vitest/dist/chunks/coverage.DM_a_rWm.js:538).

COMPONENT_TABLES (tests/setupBrowser.ts:5923) has its maximum at the dropdown table (tests/setupBrowser.ts:5961, scenarios at :5964): 88 scenarios, read directly from the runtime constant by Timeout probe in each header stdout.log:5. Therefore tests/app/browser/integration.test.ts:2168 evaluates to 10000 + 88 × 2500 = 230000 ms. The command passes --ceiling 5000 from the present COMPONENT_WAIT (tests/setupBrowser.ts:3200); its R4 column is not a newly accepted timeout or a derived U8 budget.

## Instrument commands

These are the exact queued header commands and duration-instrument invocation. The omission JSON is supplied through standard input so no additional file is written. The brief's pre-B4 exclusion governs, rather than the older U1b fixture that excluded B4 to retain the older common population. The instrument exits 0. The wider requested population gives band 1.265340; no supplied run is out of band. This differs from the two-run U7a report that excluded task75-u7-probe-1.

## Deviations

| Expected | Found and evidence | Done or not | One hypothesis |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Shadow both imports in Showcase.test.ts | The file imports only waitForCondition (restored source :2); helpers reach animations at setupBrowser.ts:1594, :1949, :1971. | Wrapped the existing direct condition import and both setup imports; added a shared temporary recorder and once-per-test logging. | None needed. |
| Viewport name where the test has one | Showcase test registrations have no named viewport variant; some tests loop widths. | Used app:browser as the nonempty variant. | None needed. |
| Instrument reports every Settle probe family | renderTitle at durations.ts:81 maps every family to a statechart; isEligible at :401 rejects non-statechart tests. | Left the off-limits instrument unchanged; appended full labelled non-statechart and header supplements with R3/slack marks. Native instrument coverage remains incomplete. | Hard-coded title mapping is the observed cause. |
| Counts denote distinct recorded waits | durations.ts:94-104 reads journey text and stdout; the U7a statechart entries occur in both. | Retained generated counts; supplemental counts use stdout once. Duplicate copies change counts, not extrema or R3. | None needed. |
| R3 available for each measured group | Generated rows include 569 unavailable figures; supplement includes 151 U7a and one header unavailable figure under the positive-minimum/two-run rule. | Preserved unavailable results; selected no replacement budget. | None needed. |
| R3 figures fit enclosing slack | The marked supplemental rows exceed slack, including header animation 151100 ms versus 28495.7 ms. | Reported every observed mark; U8 budget selection remains outside this probe's scope. | Mixed immediate and transition-spanning waits may inflate the max/min margin. |
| Earlier U1b example omits B4; earlier U7a excludes probe-1 by load | This brief asks for pre-B4 title omissions and the broader run population. | Passed the ten pre-B4 run/title pairs; both U7a runs are eligible under the resulting 1.265340 band. | None needed. |

The runtime Timeout probe adds only a read of COMPONENT_TABLES to the temporary probe. Both source files were restored from HEAD after the commands completed. git status --short is empty at 6a976a0b7a6c8eac2fd76bdbd828fd8814bfeb35. No commit was created. Outside launcher-produced run artifacts, only the two named output files remain.
